Um... four days straight? Like, exactly four days? I... I'm really not sure I could put an exact number on it like that. I don't want to give you the wrong answer.

Um... well, if we're looking at the bigger picture, rather than just counting a specific four days... I suppose this whole feeling has been building for about a month now?

I just want to make sure I'm giving you the right information.\n\nDoctor: Can you recall if there was a time when your mood was unusually high, euphoric, or irritable for several days in a row, where you felt like you didn’t need sleep or were more impulsive than usual?\n\nPatient: Um... unusually high or euphoric? ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
I... I know you've asked me this a few times now, and I really, really want to give you the exact answer you're looking for. I'm sorry if I'm being frustrating. I'm really trying my best to be a good patient for you.

It's just... I honestly can't think of a specific four-day stretch like that. I know you're trying to pin down a timeline, and I feel terrible that I can't just say "yes, it was Tuesday through Friday." I just haven't been keeping track of my mood in that way, you know? It all just feels like one long, productive blur lately. I hope it's okay that I don't have a better answer for you on that specific detail. I'm really trying, I promise.
